\\\\\\\\nlogs/workers/worker_code_exp_001_bias_bound_mvp_matrix_v2_20260531T155006.log\\\\\\\\nlogs/workers/worker_code_plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp_gate_rmse_v3_20260531T161019.log\\\\\\\\nlogs/workers/worker_gpu_exp_002_dgp_grid_launch_20260531T154505.log\\\\\\\\ndream_registry.lock\\\\\\\\ndocs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"exit_code\\\\\\\":0,\\\\\\\"status\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"completed\\\\\\\"}}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"item.started\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"item\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"id\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"item_4\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"mcp_tool_call\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"server\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"agent-ml-research\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"tool\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"read_project_file\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"arguments\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"project\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"ec_hte_codex_a\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"file\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"ROADMAP.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"max_chars\\\\\\\":16000},\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\":null,\\\\\\\"error\\\\\\\":null,\\\\\\\"status\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"in_progress\\\\\\\"}}\\\\n{\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"item.completed\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"item\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"id\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"item_4\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"mcp_tool_call\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"server\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"agent-ml-research\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"tool\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"read_project_file\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"arguments\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"project\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"ec_hte_codex_a\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"file\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"ROADMAP.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"max_chars\\\\\\\":16000},\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":[{\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"{\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": true, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"data\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"# ROADMAP\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- naive CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", 项目目标\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- naive CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", :\\\\\\\"read_project_file\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"arguments\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"project\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"ec_hte_codex_a\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"file\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"memory.md\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"max_chars\\\\\\\":16000},\\\\\\\"result\\\\\\\":{\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\":[{\\\\\\\"type\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\",\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\":\\\\\\\"{\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": true, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"data\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"# Memory\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 执行事件记录\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- [2026-05-31] `worker_research_baseline_protocol_ec_hte_20260531T153440` 被系统标记为 failed，但核心调研产物已接收：`memory.md` 已写入领域参考/首批实验协议，artifact 已生成于 `artifacts/research/baseline_protocol_20260531.md`，manifest 已登记 `art_20260531_baseline_protocol`。失败原因是非关键收尾命令在非 git repo 目录执行 `git status` / `git diff` 报错，不影响 baseline/protocol 内容。默认不重跑同一调研 Worker；除非 Director 要求更严格 source audit。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", {\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"path\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"# Memory\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n## 执行事件记录\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\n- [2026-05-31] ec_hte_codex_a\\\\n\\\\nexperiments:\\\\n- id: plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\\\\n  claim: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\\\\n  description: >\\\\n    CPU-only semi-synthetic MVP for H1. Compare uncorrected, oracle global\\\\n    confusion correction, estimated global confusion correction, and\\\\n    stratified/subgroup correction on the positive-correlation worst-case DGP.\\\\n    This isolates structural global-correction bias from finite-sample confusion\\\\n    estimation noise.\\\\n  registry_id: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\\\\n  role: primary\\\\n  priority: P0\\\\n  gpu_budget_hours: 0\\\\n  deps: []\\\\n  matrix_targets:\\\\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\\\\n      col: uncorrected_vs_oracle_global\\\\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\\\\n      col: uncorrected_vs_estimated_global\\\\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\\\\n      col: uncorrected_gate_rmse\\\\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\\\\n      col: oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\\\\n      col: estimated_global_gate_rmse\\\\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\\\\n      col: stratified_gate_rmse\\\\n  operational_criteria:\\\\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\\\\n    secondary_metrics:\\\\n    - mean_subgroup_cate_bias\\\\n    - max_subgroup_cate_bias\\\\n    minimum_replications: 200\\\\n    success_rule: >\\\\n      In the positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity\\\\n      setting, oracle_global - uncorrected must be greater than 0 on the primary\\\\n      metric gate_rmse, with a bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. Estimated global\\\\n      is reported separately; estimated_global - oracle_global quantifies\\\\n      finite-sample confusion-estimation noise.\\\\n    diagnostic_rule: >\\\\n      mean_subgroup_cate_bias and max_subgroup_cate_bias must be reported for all\\\\n      methods as secondary diagnostics. If gate_rmse supports H1 but either bias\\\\n      diagnostic points in the opposite direction, the result is marked mixed and\\\\n      requires Director review before Stage 1.\\\\n    negative_rule: >\\\\n      If oracle_global is not worse than uncorrected under the positive-correlation\\\\n      worst-case setting, H1 is not supported and the next step is to audit DGP\\\\n      construction, correction algebra, metric definitions, and subgroup weighting\\\\n      before running Stage 1.\\\\n\\\\n- id: plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\\\\n  claim: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\\\\n  description: >\\\\n    GPU-vectorized DGP grid for H1. Systematically vary misclassification\\\\n    heterogeneity, CATE heterogeneity, subgroup count, and correlation direction;\\\\n    compare oracle global and estimated global correction against uncorrected and\\\\n    stratified/subgroup correction.\\\\n  registry_id: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\\\\n  role: primary\\\\n  priority: P0\\\\n  gpu_budget_hours: 2\\\\n  deps:\\\\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\\\\n  matrix_targets:\\\\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\\\\n      col: grid_oracle_vs_estimated_mechanism_split\\\\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\\\\n      col: bias_bound_auc_or_rank_corr\\\\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\\\\n      col: oracle_estimated_gap\\\\n  operational_criteria:\\\\n    primary_metric: global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative\\\\n    secondary_metrics:\\\\n    - bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman\\\\n    - estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\\n    minimum_replications_per_condition: 200\\\\n    success_rule: >\\\\n      Positive-correlation conditions must have at least a 20 percentage-point\\\\n      higher oracle-global-worse rate than negative-correlation controls, with a\\\\n      bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. The bias-bound diagnostic must rank\\\\n      oracle-global-worse conditions with Spearman rho >= 0.50 and bootstrap 95%\\\\n      CI lower bound above 0. Estimated-global results must be reported separately\\\\n      from oracle global to avoid conflating structural bias with\\\\n      confusion-estimation noise.\\\\n    launch_gate: >\\\\n      This experiment may only run after Director approval and GPU allocation.\\\\n      Before allocation it may only prepare code, configs, dry-runs, and proposal\\\\n      artifacts.\\\\n\\\\n- id: plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation\\\\n  claim: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\\\\n  description: >\\\\n    Stage 1 correction ablation comparing global, arm-specific, per-subgroup\\\\n    independent, and hierarchical/partial-pooling correction under matched expert\\\\n    label budgets.\\\\n  role: follow_up\\\\n  priority: P1\\\\n  gpu_budget_hours: 4\\\\n  deps:\\\\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\\\\n  - plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\\\\n  matrix_targets:\\\\n  - target_id: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: low_budget\\\\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_global\\\\n    - row: low_budget\\\\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_per_subgroup\\\\n  - target_id: T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION\\\\n    cells:\\\\n    - row: budget_low\\\\n      col: global\\\\n    - row: budget_low\\\\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\\\\n    - row: budget_low\\\\n      col: partial_pooling\\\\n    - row: budget_mid\\\\n      col: global\\\\n    - row: budget_mid\\\\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\\\\n    - row: budget_mid\\\\n      col: partial_pooling\\\\n  operational_criteria:\\\\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\\\\n    secondary_metrics:\\\\n    - pehe\\\\n    - mean_subgroup_cate_bias\\\\n    success_rule: >\\\\n      At low expert-label budget, partial pooling must beat global correction on\\\\n      gate_rmse with bootstrap 95% CI upper bound below 0 for\\\\n      partial_pooling_minus_global. Compare uncorrected, oracle global\n    confusion correction, estimated global confusion correction, and\n    stratified/subgroup correction on the positive-correlation worst-case DGP.\n    This isolates structural global-correction bias from finite-sample confusion\n    estimation noise.\n  registry_id: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n  role: primary\n  priority: P0\n  gpu_budget_hours: 0\n  deps: []\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n    cells:\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n      col: uncorrected_vs_oracle_global\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n      col: uncorrected_vs_estimated_global\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\n    cells:\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: uncorrected_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: oracle_global_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: estimated_global_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: stratified_gate_rmse\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\n    secondary_metrics:\n    - mean_subgroup_cate_bias\n    - max_subgroup_cate_bias\n    minimum_replications: 200\n    success_rule: >\n      In the positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity\n      setting, oracle_global - uncorrected must be greater than 0 on the primary\n      metric gate_rmse, with a bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. Estimated global\n      is reported separately; estimated_global - oracle_global quantifies\n      finite-sample confusion-estimation noise.\n    diagnostic_rule: >\n      mean_subgroup_cate_bias and max_subgroup_cate_bias must be reported for all\n      methods as secondary diagnostics. If gate_rmse supports H1 but either bias\n      diagnostic points in the opposite direction, the result is marked mixed and\n      requires Director review before Stage 1.\n    negative_rule: >\n      If oracle_global is not worse than uncorrected under the positive-correlation\n      worst-case setting, H1 is not supported and the next step is to audit DGP\n      construction, correction algebra, metric definitions, and subgroup weighting\n      before running Stage 1.\n\n- id: plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\n  claim: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n  description: >\n    GPU-vectorized DGP grid for H1. Systematically vary misclassification\n    heterogeneity, CATE heterogeneity, subgroup count, and correlation direction;\n    compare oracle global and estimated global correction against uncorrected and\n    stratified/subgroup correction.\n  registry_id: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\n  role: primary\n  priority: P0\n  gpu_budget_hours: 2\n  deps:\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n    cells:\n    - row: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\n      col: grid_oracle_vs_estimated_mechanism_split\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\n    cells:\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\n      col: bias_bound_auc_or_rank_corr\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\n      col: oracle_estimated_gap\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative\n    secondary_metrics:\n    - bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman\n    - estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\n    minimum_replications_per_condition: 200\n    success_rule: >\n      Positive-correlation conditions must have at least a 20 percentage-point\n      higher oracle-global-worse rate than negative-correlation controls, with a\n      bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. The bias-bound diagnostic must rank\n      oracle-global-worse conditions with Spearman rho >= 0.50 and bootstrap 95%\n      CI lower bound above 0. Estimated-global results must be reported separately\n      from oracle global to avoid conflating structural bias with\n      confusion-estimation noise.\n    launch_gate: >\n      This experiment may only run after Director approval and GPU allocation.\n      Before allocation it may only prepare code, configs, dry-runs, and proposal\n      artifacts.\n\n- id: plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation\n  claim: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\n  description: >\n    Stage 1 correction ablation comparing global, arm-specific, per-subgroup\n    independent, and hierarchical/partial-pooling correction under matched expert\n    label budgets.\n  role: follow_up\n  priority: P1\n  gpu_budget_hours: 4\n  registry_id: exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation\n  deps:\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\n  - plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\n    cells:\n    - row: low_budget\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_global\n    - row: low_budget\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_per_subgroup\n  - target_id: T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION\n    cells:\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: global\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: arm_specific\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: partial_pooling\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: global\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: arm_specific\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: partial_pooling\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\n",
